149: // credits to: https://github.com/astoniocom/control_style/blob/master/lib/src/decorated_input_border.dart
150: class DecoratedInputBorder extends InputBorder {
151:   DecoratedInputBorder({required this.child, required this.shadow})
152:     : super(borderSide: child.borderSide);
153: 
154:   final InputBorder child;
155: 
156:   final BoxShadow shadow;
157: 
158:   @override
159:   bool get isOutline => child.isOutline;
160: 
161:   @override
162:   Path getInnerPath(Rect rect, {TextDirection? textDirection}) =>
163:       child.getInnerPath(rect, textDirection: textDirection);
164: 
165:   @override
166:   Path getOuterPath(Rect rect, {TextDirection? textDirection}) =>
167:       child.getOuterPath(rect, textDirection: textDirection);
168: 
169:   @override
170:   EdgeInsetsGeometry get dimensions => child.dimensions;
171: 
172:   @override
173:   InputBorder copyWith({
174:     BorderSide? borderSide,
175:     InputBorder? child,
176:     BoxShadow? shadow,
177:     bool? isOutline,
178:   }) {
179:     return DecoratedInputBorder(
180:       child: (child ?? this.child).copyWith(borderSide: borderSide),
181:       shadow: shadow ?? this.shadow,
182:     );
183:   }
184: 
185:   @override
186:   ShapeBorder scale(double t) {
187:     final scalledChild = child.scale(t);
188: 
189:     return DecoratedInputBorder(
190:       child: scalledChild is InputBorder ? scalledChild : child,
191:       shadow: BoxShadow.lerp(null, shadow, t)!,
192:     );
193:   }
194: 
195:   @override
196:   void paint(
197:     Canvas canvas,
198:     Rect rect, {
199:     double? gapStart,
200:     double gapExtent = 0.0,
201:     double gapPercentage = 0.0,
202:     TextDirection? textDirection,
203:   }) {
204:     final clipPath = Path()
205:       ..addRect(const Rect.fromLTWH(-5000, -5000, 10000, 10000))
206:       ..addPath(getInnerPath(rect), Offset.zero)
207:       ..fillType = PathFillType.evenOdd;
208:     canvas.clipPath(clipPath);
209: 
210:     final Paint paint = shadow.toPaint();
211:     final Rect bounds = rect.shift(shadow.offset).inflate(shadow.spreadRadius);
212: 
213:     canvas.drawPath(getOuterPath(bounds), paint);
214: 
215:     child.paint(
216:       canvas,
217:       rect,
218:       gapStart: gapStart,
219:       gapExtent: gapExtent,
220:       gapPercentage: gapPercentage,
221:       textDirection: textDirection,
222:     );
223:   }
224: }

16:       // must not trigger a redirect.
17:       if (next.isLoading) return;
18:       next.when(
19:         data: (data) {
20:           if (!data.isAuthenticated) redirect(context, "/welcome");
21:         },
22:         error: (e, __) {
23:           if (e is ErrorWithCode) {
24:             if (["not_authenticated", "user_not_found"].contains(e.code)) {
25:               return redirect(context, "/welcome");
26:             }
27:           }
28:         },
29:         loading: () => null,
30:       );
31:     });
32: 
33:     return child;
34:   }
35: }

19: 
20: class _LoadingPageState extends ConsumerState<LoadingPage> {
21:   @override
22:   void initState() {
23:     super.initState();
24:     // Read (and thus initialize) homeProvider exactly once, before build's
25:     // ref.listen subscribes. On a cold start this kicks off the fetch;
26:     // invalidating on top of it would fire a duplicate request, since
27:     // listening initializes the provider anyway.
28:     final initial = ref.read(homeProvider);
29:     // Only when /loading is re-entered with an already settled (stale)
30:     // result — e.g. after registering — force a fresh fetch after the first
31:     // frame. (Modifying providers during build/initState is not allowed.)
32:     if (!initial.isLoading) {
33:       WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
34:         if (mounted) {
35:           ref.invalidate(homeProvider);
36:         }
37:       });
38:     }
39:   }
40: 
41:   /// Waits for authProvider to resolve (secure-storage token read), then
42:   /// sends a logged-in user into the app even though the fetch failed,
43:   /// or to /welcome when there's no session at all.
44:   Future<void> _redirectAfterAuthResolves() async {
45:     try {
46:       final auth = await ref.read(authProvider.future);
47:       if (!mounted) return;
48:       redirect(context, auth.isAuthenticated ? "/" : "/welcome");
49:     } catch (_) {
50:       if (!mounted) return;
51:       redirect(context, "/welcome");
52:     }
53:   }
54: 
55:   @override
56:   Widget build(BuildContext context) {
57:     ref.listen<AsyncValue<Home>>(homeProvider, (prev, next) {
58:       // While refreshing, AsyncValue keeps the PREVIOUS error/value and
59:       // when() would replay it (skipLoadingOnRefresh). Only react to
60:       // settled states, or a stale user_not_in_house error would bounce
61:       // the user to /create_home even after a successful create+refetch.
62:       if (next.isLoading) return;
63: 
64:       next.when(
65:         data: (_) => redirect(context, "/"),
66:         error: (e, _) {
67:           if (e is ErrorWithCode &&
68:               ["not_authenticated", "user_not_found"].contains(e.code)) {
69:             ref.read(authProvider.notifier).reset();
70:             redirect(context, "/register");
71:           } else if (e is ErrorWithCode && e.code == "user_not_in_house") {
72:             redirect(context, "/create_home");
73:           } else {
74:             // Network/server error (e.g. no internet, server down): a
75:             // logged-in user must NOT be kicked to the welcome/login
76:             // screens. authProvider may still be resolving (the secure
77:             // storage read is async and slower than a connection refusal),
78:             // so wait for it before deciding where to go.
79:             _redirectAfterAuthResolves();
80:           }
81:         },
82:         loading: () => null,
83:       );
84:     });
85:     return Scaffold(
86:       backgroundColor: context.colors.surface,
87:       body: Center(child: HTitle(text: "Sto Caricando")),
88:     );
89:   }

_Thinking:_

I now have a comprehensive view of the app's design. Let me also check the project-notes folder for any design references, and check the assets folder to see what's there. Also check doc.md briefly for context. Let me also verify the theme details — I noticed:

Key design observations:

**Theme:**
- Font: Poppins (400, 500, 600, 700)
- Light mode only
- Colors:
  - primary: `0xFFFFC342` (warm yellow/amber) — "Homies yellow"
  - onPrimary: `0xFF202020` (near-black)
  - secondary: `0xFFE5E5E5` (light gray) — secondary buttons
  - onSecondary: `0xCC202020` (near-black at 80% opacity)
  - surface: `0xFFF6F6F6` (app background, very light gray)
  - onSurface: `0xFF202020`
  - surfaceContainer: `0xFFFFFFFF` (cards/inputs)
  - error: `0xFFB94E4E` (muted red)
  - onError: `0xFFFFFFFF`
  - Offline card uses `0xFFE94D4D` (brighter red) — static const
  - Unchecked checkbox border: `0xFFAAAAAA`
  - Gray icon in invite: Colors.grey

**Typography scale:**
- displayLarge: 52 bold (welcome hero "Homies")
- displaySmall: 20 w500 (page subtitles)
- headlineLarge: 42 bold height 1.0 (page H1 + invite code)
- headlineMedium: 28 bold (section titles)
- headlineSmall: 24 bold (sheet titles)
- titleLarge: 16 w500 (app bar title)
- titleMedium: 16 bold
- titleSmall: 14 w600 (labels, error text, card messages)
- bodyLarge: 16 w400 (body text, input text)
- bodyMedium: 14 w400
- labelLarge: 16 w600 (button text)
- labelMedium: 14 w600 (calendar days)

**Spacing system:**
- 4, 8, 12, 16, 24, 32, 36, 48, 80
- Screen padding: 24 all around
- Between title & subtitle: 12
- Between subtitle & form: 24
- Between inputs: 8
- Between primary & secondary buttons: 12
- List item bottom spacing: 12

**Radii:**
- 8: small buttons (nav items, icon buttons, retry icon, inner calendar cells... wait calendar inner uses 12)
- 12: calendar day cells
- 16: cards, inputs, main buttons
- 24: bottom sheets top radius

**Shadows (signature soft shadow):**
- BoxShadow(color: onSurface @ 0.25 alpha, blurRadius: 2, offset: Offset(0,1)) — everywhere: buttons, cards, inputs (via DecoratedInputBorder), navbar, app bar (shadowColor onSurface 0.25)

**Components (H prefix):**
- HButton: full-width 48h, radius 16, soft shadow, loading state, icon support
- HLabeledInput: label (titleSmall, left padding 12, bottom 2), white fill, radius 16, soft shadow, lucide prefix icon 24, 48 min height, obscure toggle suffix
- HTitle: bold title + yellow "." suffix — signature element
- HNavBar: 4 items (Home, Todo, Calendar, Wallet), white bg, soft shadow, 80-wide icon buttons radius 8, selected = primary bg + onPrimary icon, unselected = secondary + onSecondary
- HTaskTile: white card, radius 16, custom checkbox 18px circle 2px border, leading 40w, avatar 36 trailing
- HAvatar: SVG generated face avatar, size param (40 navbar, 36 task tile, 60 members, 120 settings)
- HWeekCalendar: white card, radius 16, day cells radius 12, selected primary
- HOfflineCard: red 0xFFE94D4D bg, white text/icons, radius 16, retry icon
- Guards: AuthGuard, HomeGuard
- SettingsAvatarButton: avatar 40 in app bar actions with right padding 24

**Icons:** flutter_lucide (Lucide icon set), 24 default, 20 smaller
- house, list_todo, calendar, wallet (nav)
- user_round, lock (auth inputs)
- eye/eye_closed (obscure)
- trash, square_pen (task actions)
- plus (new task)
- wifi_off, cloud_off, rotate_cw (offline)
- copy (invite)

**Page patterns:**
- Scaffold: AppBar with HTitle "Homies" (titleLarge style) — app bar always says "Homies" with yellow dot, page identity comes from body HTitle
- AppBar style: scrolledUnderElevation: 1, surfaceTintColor: surface, backgroundColor: surface, shadowColor: onSurface@0.25
- backgroundColor: surface
- Main pages: bottomNavigationBar HNavBar, body Column [offline card?, Expanded(RefreshIndicator(content))]
- Content pages: SafeArea + Center + SingleChildScrollView(padding 24) + Column centered
- Auth/form pages: HTitle (headlineLarge, may include \n), 12, subtitle (displaySmall), 24, inputs, 24, primary HButton, 12, secondary HButton
- Loading: CircularProgressIndicator center
- Error handling: error message in titleSmall red above button; button becomes "Try Again"; snackbar for task errors
- Offline/error: HOfflineCard at top with message + retry
- Empty/loading/error `when()` pattern with Riverpod AsyncValue
- Pull-to-refresh with AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics
- Bouncing scroll physics everywhere (AppScrollBehavior)
- GestureDetector unfocus on tap outside (todo page, HButton unfocuses before callback)
- Bottom sheets: showModalBottomSheet, isScrollControlled, showDragHandle, radius 24 top, bg surface, barrier onSurface.withAlpha(120), padding LTRB(24, 12, 24, 24)
- Confirmation flows: destructive = error color button, cancel = secondary; loading state swaps button content
- Copy tone: playful, informal English ("Do you still remember your password?", "This could be permanent, or it could be not. Who knows.", "Do you know these folks?", "You and your homies have never been so organized.")
- Hints are playful: "Splarpo", "Pa$$w0rd", "Gli Splarponi", "XXXXXX"
- The app language is English (with Italian notes/comments)

**Avatar:** procedural SVG faces - circle bg + eyes + bezier mouth; colors without # prefix in model ("999", "424242")

**Typography:**
| Role | Style | Size/Weight | Usage |
- Hero: displayLarge 52/bold — welcome screen brand
- Page H1: headlineLarge 42/bold/height 1.0 — page main title (HTitle), invite code
- Section title: headlineMedium 28/bold — "Today's Tasks"
- Sheet title: headlineSmall 24/bold — bottom sheet headings
- App bar title: titleLarge 16/w500 — always "Homies."
- Subtitle: displaySmall 20/w500 — page subtitles under HTitle
- Input label: titleSmall 14/w600 — labels, error messages, offline card text
- Body: bodyLarge 16/w400 — body text, input content, task text, hints (with color override)
- bodyMedium 14/w400 — smaller body
- Button: labelLarge 16/w600 — HButton text
- Calendar labels: labelMedium 14/w600
- titleMedium 16/bold — defined but seemingly unused? Let me check... I didn't see it used. I'll note it's reserved/unused.

**Spacing:**
- Base unit 4; common: 8, 12, 16, 24, 32, 36, 48
- Screen margin: 24 (all sides)
- Title→subtitle: 12
- Subtitle→content: 24
- Between inputs: 8
- Primary→secondary button: 12
- List tiles gap: 12
- Card inner padding: 16 (offline card, week calendar 8... hmm h_week_calendar uses 8 all). Task tile: padding all 2 wrapper (shadow workaround) with ListTile content.
- Sheet padding: LTRB(24, 12, 24, 24)

**Radii:**
- 8 — small/icon buttons, nav items, retry icon tap target, inner elements
- 12 — calendar day cells
- 16 — buttons, inputs, cards
- 24 — bottom sheet top corners
- Circle — checkbox, avatars

**Shadow (signature):**
```dart
BoxShadow(
  color: context.colors.onSurface.withValues(alpha: 0.25),
  spreadRadius: 0,
  blurRadius: 2,
  offset: Offset(0, 1),
)
```
Also AppBar: shadowColor: onSurface@0.25, scrolledUnderElevation: 1.

**Icons:**
- flutter_lucide only (no Material icons for UI); sizes 24 default, 20 compact, 22 retry.
- snake_case icon names (Lucide convention)

**Buttons:**
- HButton: height 48, full width default, radius 16, shadow on, TextButton base, unfocuses before callback, loading swaps content for 24x24 CircularProgressIndicator strokeWidth 3, icon+text gap 8.
- Variants: primary (primary/onPrimary), secondary (secondary/onSecondary), destructive (error/onError), compact icon button (48x48 radius 8 no shadow), nav item (80 wide radius 8 no shadow), inline small (36x36 radius 8 iconSize 20 no shadow — NewTaskTile plus)

**Inputs:**
- HLabeledInput: label above (titleSmall, padding left 12 bottom 2), field: white fill, radius 16, soft shadow (DecoratedInputBorder), prefix Lucide icon 24 in 48 box (padding l12 t12 b12 r4), min height 48, dense, contentPadding left 12, hint bodyLarge onSecondary@0.6, error titleSmall red, suffix eye toggle 48x48 for obscurable, bodyLarge text.
- Bare inline TextField (no border) for in-card editing (NewTaskTile, TaskSheet edit): hint "New Task...", border none.

**Cards:**
- White (surfaceContainer), radius 16, soft shadow, padding 16 typical (offline card 16 all; task tile uses 2px padding wrapper + ListTile — a shadow-clipping workaround)
- Note: the `padding: EdgeInsets.all(2)` wrapper exists so the ListTile's ink splash doesn't overflow the rounded corners / shadow region.

**Navbar:**
- 4 tabs: Home (house), Todo (list_todo), Calendar (calendar), Wallet (wallet); URLs /, /todo, /calendar, /wallet
- White bg, shadow, vertical+horizontal 12 padding, SafeArea, spaceEvenly, 80-wide buttons radius 8 no shadow; active primary/onPrimary, inactive secondary/onSecondary

**App bar (every page):**
```dart
AppBar(
  title: HTitle(text: "Homies", style: context.texts.titleLarge),
  actions: const [SettingsAvatarButton()], // when logged in
  automaticallyImplyLeading: false, // when no back needed
  scrolledUnderElevation: 1,
  surfaceTintColor: context.colors.surface,
  backgroundColor: context.colors.surface,
  shadowColor: context.colors.onSurface.withValues(alpha: 0.25),
)
```
- Page identity is NOT in the app bar — the body's HTitle carries it. App bar always shows brand "Homies."
- Settings avatar: 40px avatar, right padding 24, pushes to /settings

**HTitle:** text + yellow period. Used for app bar (titleLarge), page H1 (headlineLarge), hero (displayLarge). Supports \n.

**Page templates:**
1. Main tab page (home, todo): Scaffold + brand AppBar (+avatar) + HNavBar + Column[optional HOfflineCard (24 top padding), Expanded RefreshIndicator + content]. Content ListView padding 24.
2. Flow/form page: Scaffold + brand AppBar + SafeArea + Center + SingleChildScrollView(padding 24) + Column(center) with HTitle / subtitle 12 / inputs 8 apart / primary CTA / 12 / secondary alternate CTA.
3. Loading: centered spinner or HTitle "Sto Caricando".
4. Modal: bottom sheet recipe.

**States:**
- Riverpod AsyncValue.when(data/loading/error)
- Loading: full-width HButton loading:true replaces CTA; or centered spinner for page content
- Error in forms: red titleSmall message above CTA, CTA text becomes "Try Again"
- Page-level error/offline: HOfflineCard under app bar with retry, body kept scrollable for pull-to-refresh
- Optimistic UI: task toggle updates immediately, reverts on failure + snackbar
- PopScope blocks back during loading

**Snackbars:** default SnackBar for transient task errors.

**Bottom sheet recipe:**
```dart
showModalBottomSheet(
  isScrollControlled: true,
  showDragHandle: true,
  shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(24))),
  backgroundColor: context.colors.surface,
  barrierColor: context.colors.onSurface.withAlpha(120),
)
```
inner: SafeArea + SingleChildScrollView(padding bottom viewInsets) + padding LTRB(24,12,24,24); headlineSmall title; destructive confirm = error button first, cancel secondary.

**Motion/feel:**
- BouncingScrollPhysics everywhere (AppScrollBehavior)
- Pull-to-refresh always available (AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics)
- InkWell ripples inside cards (ListTile default), circular reveal radius matches container

**Copy tone:**
- English, informal, playful, sometimes snarky: "Do you still remember your password?", "This could be permanent, or it could be not. Who knows.", "Do you know these folks?", "You and your homies have never been so organized.", "Write something...", "New Task..."
- Playful placeholder hints: "Splarpo", "Pa$$w0rd", "Gli Splarponi", "XXXXXX"
- Sentence case for buttons and titles; "Homies." with period
- Human words over system words

**Avatar system:**
- Procedural SVG: circle bg + 2 square eyes + bezier mouth stroke; colors are hex-without-# strings from API; sizes 36 (task tile), 40 (app bar), 60 (member list), 100 default, 120 (settings)
- Fallback: sadGrayAvatar (gray #999 bg, #424242 face, sad mouth)

**Naming conventions:**
- Components: H prefix (HButton, HTitle...), files h_*.dart snake_case matching
- Non-H helpers allowed when tied to a page (SettingsAvatarButton, guards, NewTaskTile)
- context.colors / context.texts via extension

**Rules for new elements (checklist):**
- Use theme tokens, never raw colors (except documented one-offs)
- Lucide icons only
- radius from {8, 12, 16, 24}
- soft shadow recipe
- spacing multiples of 4, prefer {8, 12, 16, 24, 48}
- white cards on #F6F6F6
- build loading/error into every async surface
- English playful copy
- H prefix + snake_case file
- etc.

Design DNA at a glance\n\n- **Friendly, playful, slightly irreverent.** A shared-housing app for\n  \"homies\", not a corporate tool. Rounded shapes, warm yellow, soft shadows,\n  cheeky copy.\n- **Flat colors + one soft shadow.** No gradients, no glassmorphism, no\n  layered elevation. Depth comes from a single subtle drop shadow used\n  consistently everywhere.\n- **White cards floating on a light-gray background.**\n- **Light mode only.** No dark theme exists; do not add one without a\n  project-level decision.\n- **Yellow is the accent, not the background.** The brand yellow appears as\n  the dot after titles, on primary buttons, and on selected states.\n- **Every title ends with a yellow dot** (the `HTitle` signature).\n\n---\n\n## 2. Color system\n\n| Token | Hex | Role |\n|---|---|---|\n| `primary` | `#FFC342` | Homies yellow. Primary buttons, selected nav item, selected calendar day, checked checkbox, the `HTitle` dot. |\n| `onPrimary` | `#202020` | Text/icons on primary. |\n| `secondary` | `#E5E5E5` | Light gray. Secondary (\"quiet\") buttons, unselected nav items. |\n| `onSecondary` | `#CC202020` | Text/icons on secondary (≈ 80% black). |\n| `surface` | `#F6F6F6` | App background, app bar background, bottom sheet background, unselected calendar day cells. |\n| `onSurface` | `#202020` | Default text/icons, checkbox borders when checked, shadows (derived, see below). |\n| `surfaceContainer` | `#FFFFFF` | Cards, input fields, navbar — anything that \"floats\" on the background. |\n| `error` | `#B94E4E` | Muted red. Error messages, destructive buttons (\"Delete\", \"Leave Home\"). |\n| `onError` | `#FFFFFF` | Text/icons on error. |\n\n### 2.1 Off-token colors (documented exceptions)\n\nThese raw colors exist in code outside the theme. Reuse them for the same\npurpose; do not introduce new raw colors.\n\n| Hex | Where | Purpose |\n|---|---|---|\n| `#E94D4D` | `HOfflineCard.errorBackground` | Brighter red for the offline/error banner (louder than `error` on purpose — it must grab attention). |\n| `#AAAAAA` | `HTaskTile` unchecked checkbox border | Disabled/unchecked border tone. |\n| `Colors.transparent` | checkbox fill (unchecked), ink overlays | — |\n| `Colors.white` | `HOfflineCard` icons/text | Same value as `onError`; write `onError`-style intent as \"white on red\". |\n\n### 2.2 Opacity conventions (derived from `onSurface` / `onSecondary`)\n\n| Opacity | Used for |\n|---|---|\n| `onSurface @ 0.25` | Every shadow (see §6). Also the app bar `shadowColor`. |\n| `onSurface @ 0.5` (`withValues(alpha: 0.5)`) | Completed-task text + strikethrough color. |\n| `onSecondary @ 0.6` | Input hint text. |\n| `onSurface @ withAlpha(120)` (≈ 47%) | Bottom-sheet barrier color. |\n| `secondary @ withAlpha(120)` | Disabled-feeling cancel button while an operation is in flight (see `LeaveConfirmationModal`). |\n\n### 2.3 Rules\n\n1. **Never hardcode a hex in a widget.** Use `context.colors.<token>` (the\n   `ThemeContext` extension from `lib/extensions/theme_extension.dart`). New\n   colors go into `theme.dart` first.\n2. Text is always `onSurface`, `onPrimary`, `onSecondary`, `onError`, or\n   `error` — never `primary` (yellow fails contrast for text; it is a fill\n   color, and the `HTitle` dot is its only \"text\" use).\n3. Contrast pairs are fixed: `primary↔onPrimary`, `secondary↔onSecondary`,\n   `error↔onError`, white card ↔ `onSurface`.\n\n---\n\n## 3. Typography\n\n**Font: Poppins** — bundled at weights 400 (Regular), 500 (Medium),\n600 (SemiBold), 700 (Bold). Declared in `pubspec.yaml`, set globally in\n`theme.dart`. Never use another font family, and never a weight outside\n400–700.\n\nAccess via `context.texts.<style>`; override with `.copyWith(...)` only for\ncolor/decoration, not size.\n\n### 3.1 Scale\n\n| Style | Size / weight | Line height | Role |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| `displayLarge` | 52 / bold | default | Splash/hero brand title (welcome screen only). |\n| `displaySmall` | 20 / w500 | default | **Page subtitle** — the friendly line under every `HTitle`. |\n| `headlineLarge` | 42 / bold | 1.0 | **Page main title** (`HTitle` default) and big display values (invite code). |\n| `headlineMedium` | 28 / bold | default | Section title inside a page (\"Today's Tasks\"). |\n| `headlineSmall` | 24 / bold | default | Bottom-sheet / modal titles. |\n| `titleLarge` | 16 / w500 | default | **App bar title** (\"Homies.\"), user name on settings. |\n| `titleMedium` | 16 / bold | default | *(defined, currently unused — reserve for 16px bold inline titles).* |\n| `titleSmall` | 14 / w600 | default | **Input labels, inline error messages, banner text.** |\n| `bodyLarge` | 16 / w400 | default | **Default body text**, input field content, hints, task text. |\n| `bodyMedium` | 14 / w400 | default | *(defined, currently unused — reserve for dense secondary body text).* |\n| `labelLarge` | 16 / w600 | default | **Button text** (`HButton`). |\n| `labelMedium` | 14 / w600 | default | Small UI labels (calendar day letters). |\n\n### 3.2 Rules\n\n1. A page hierarchy is: `HTitle` (headlineLarge) → subtitle (displaySmall) →\n   section titles (headlineMedium) → body (bodyLarge). Do not invent levels.\n2. Input hints use `bodyLarge` **with the hint color override** (§2.2), not a\n   smaller size.\n3. Error messages are always `titleSmall` + `error` color.\n4. Buttons are always `labelLarge`; never resize button text.\n5. Multi-word H1s break with an explicit `\\n` in the string\n   (\"Welcome\\ninto Homies\"), not automatic wrapping, so the break is witty.\n\n---\n\n## 4. Iconography\n\n- **Package: `flutter_lucide`** (Lucide set). Icon identifiers are\n  **snake_case** (`LucideIcons.list_todo`, `LucideIcons.user_round`).\n- Material icons are only for system widgets that draw their own (e.g. the\n  progress indicator); never pick a Material icon for UI semantics.\n- Sizes: **24** is the default (inputs, buttons, tiles); **20** for compact\n  spots (offline card, small icon buttons); **22** for the retry icon.\n- Icon color follows the container's `on*` color.\n- Icons in use today: `house`, `list_todo`, `calendar`, `wallet` (nav);\n  `user_round`, `lock` (auth inputs); `eye` / `eye_closed` (obscure toggle);\n  `trash`, `square_pen` (task actions); `plus` (new task); `wifi_off`,\n  `cloud_off` (offline); `rotate_cw` (retry); `copy` (invite code).\n  Stay within the Lucide style: 2px stroke, rounded caps, no filled icons.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Layout & spacing\n\n**Base unit: 4.** Allowed steps: 4, 8, 12, 16, 24, 32, 36, 48, 80. If a value\nisn't on this ladder, reconsider it.\n\n| Distance | Value |\n|---|---|\n| Screen/page margin | **24** all sides (`EdgeInsets.all(24)`) |\n| `HTitle` → subtitle | **12** |\n| Subtitle → form/content block | **24** |\n| Between input fields | **8** (plus each label's 2px bottom padding) |\n| Primary button → secondary button | **12** |\n| Between list tiles / cards | **12** |\n| Section title → content (home) | **12** |\n| H1 → content blocks (home) | **32–48** (roomier at the top of a page) |\n| Card inner padding | **16** (offline card), **8** (week calendar), **2+ListTile** (task tile) |\n| Bottom-sheet inner padding | `fromLTRB(24, 12, 24, 24)` |\n| Icon → adjacent text inside a button | **8** |\n| Small icon → text rows (offline card, invite) | **12** |\n\n### 5.1 Page structure conventions\n\n- **Tab pages** (home, todo): `Scaffold` with brand app bar, content\n  `Column`, `HNavBar` as `bottomNavigationBar`. Content lists pad 24; the\n  horizontal padding may sit on the inner `Column` (todo) or the `ListView`\n  (home) — keep 24 either way.\n- **Flow pages** (auth, create/join home): `SafeArea → Center →\n  SingleChildScrollView(padding: 24) → Column(crossAxisAlignment:\n  start, mainAxisAlignment: center)`. The form is vertically centered on\n  large screens and scrolls on small ones.\n- Full-width stretch: cards and buttons use `width: double.infinity`.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Elevation — the one shadow\n\nEvery floating element (buttons, cards, inputs, navbar, app bar) uses the\n**same** soft shadow. This is the app's signature depth cue.\n\n```dart\nBoxShadow(\n  color: context.colors.onSurface.withValues(alpha: 0.25),\n  spreadRadius: 0,\n  blurRadius: 2,\n  offset: Offset(0, 1),\n)\n```\n\nApp bar equivalent:\n\n```dart\nAppBar(\n  scrolledUnderElevation: 1,\n  surfaceTintColor: context.colors.surface, // no M3 tint\n  backgroundColor: context.colors.surface,\n  shadowColor: context.colors.onSurface.withValues(alpha: 0.25),\n)\n```\n\nRules:\n1. One shadow per element. No stacked shadows, no elevation numbers beyond\n   this recipe.\n2. Compact buttons inside another surface (nav items, `NewTaskTile`'s plus,\n   sheet action squares) set `shadow: false`.\n3. A shadow that gets clipped by rounded corners goes on an outer\n   `Container` while the inner widget keeps the tap ripple (see the task\n   tile's 2px-padding wrapper trick).\n\n---\n\n## 7. Shape / corner radius\n\n| Radius | Used on |\n|---|---|\n| **8** | Small & icon buttons, nav item buttons, retry tap target |\n| **12** | Calendar day cells |\n| **16** | **Default**: cards, inputs, standard buttons, tap targets that wrap content |\n| **24** (top corners only) | Bottom sheets: `BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(24))` |\n| **circle / square** | Checkbox is an 18px circle; avatar eyes are rounded squares (SVG) |\n\nNew element? Start at 16. Go to 8 only for compact controls, 24 only for\nsheet-style surfaces. The navbar container itself is square (radius 0) — it\nspans the screen edge-to-edge.\n\n---\n\n## 8. Component catalog\n\nAll shared UI lives in `lib/ui/components/` and is prefixed **`H`** with a\nmatching `h_*.dart` filename. Reuse these before building anything new.\n\n### 8.1 `HButton` — the only button\n\nFull-width by default (`width: double.infinity`), **48** tall, radius 16,\nsoft shadow, `TextButton` under the hood.\n\nKey API: `text`, `icon`, `iconSize` (24), `color` *(required — variant is\nexplicit, not named)*, `textColor`, `width`, `height`, `borderRadius`,\n`shadow`, `loading`, `loadingColor`, `onPressed`.\n\nBehavior baked in — preserve it in any variant:\n- **Unfocuses the keyboard** (`FocusScope.of(context).unfocus()`) before\n  calling `onPressed`.\n- `loading: true` swaps content for a 24×24 `CircularProgressIndicator`,\n  strokeWidth 3, color `loadingColor ?? onPrimary`.\n- Icon + text are centered in a row with an 8px gap.\n\nVariants in use (copy these recipes):\n\n| Variant | color / textColor | Other |\n|---|---|---|\n| Primary CTA | `primary` / `onPrimary` | defaults |\n| Secondary / quiet CTA | `secondary` / `onSecondary` | defaults |\n| Destructive CTA | `error` / `onError` | defaults |\n| Sheet action square | `secondary` / `onSecondary` | `width: 48` (icon only) |\n| Inline compact (e.g. add-task +) | `primary` | `width: 36, height: 36, borderRadius: 8, iconSize: 20, shadow: false` |\n| Nav item | selected: `primary`/`onPrimary`, else `secondary`/`onSecondary` | `width: 80, borderRadius: 8, shadow: false`, icon only |\n\nAsync CTA pattern: `loading` replaces the button content — the button keeps\nits size so the layout never jumps.\n\n### 8.2 `HLabeledInput` — the only labeled text field\n\nAnatomy, top to bottom:\n1. Label: `titleSmall`, padding `EdgeInsets.only(left: 12, bottom: 2)` —\n   optically aligned with the field's inner left padding.\n2. `TextFormField`, min height 48, white fill (`surfaceContainer`), radius 16,\n   the standard soft shadow (via `DecoratedInputBorder` wrapping an\n   `OutlineInputBorder` with `borderSide: BorderSide.none`).\n3. Prefix: Lucide icon, size 24, boxed in a 48×48 area with padding\n   `left: 12, top: 12, bottom: 12, right: 4`.\n4. Suffix (only when `obscurable`): a 48×48 `InkWell` (radius 16) with\n   `eye` / `eye_closed`, shown only when the field has text.\n5. Text and hint: `bodyLarge`; hint colored `onSecondary @ 0.6`.\n6. Validation error: `titleSmall` + `error`; built-in validator rejects empty\n   (\"Cannot be empty.\").\n7. `contentPadding: EdgeInsets.only(left: 12)` and\n   `textAlignVertical: TextAlignVertical.center`.\n\nAPI: `label`, `hint`, `icon`, `controller`, `keyboardType`, `obscurable`.\n\nRules: use it for every form field; playful hints are part of the style\n(\"Splarpo\", \"Pa$$w0rd\"). For **borderless inline editing inside a card**\n(New-task tile, task edit), use a raw `TextField` with `InputBorder.none` and\nthe same hint styling instead — that's the established pattern.\n\n### 8.3 `HTitle` — the signature title\n\nBold text + a trailing yellow period, via `RichText`. Default style\n`headlineLarge`; override with `style:` (app bar uses `titleLarge`, welcome\nhero uses `displayLarge`). Never place a literal yellow dot in a string —\nuse `HTitle`. Never use a colon after a title.\n\n### 8.4 `HNavBar` — bottom tab bar\n\n- Exactly 4 tabs: Home `/` (`house`), Todo `/todo` (`list_todo`),\n  Calendar `/calendar` (`calendar`), Wallet `/wallet` (`wallet`).\n  Tab order and icons are fixed.\n- White container (`surfaceContainer`) with the soft shadow, padding 12 all\n  around, `SafeArea` inside, items spread `spaceEvenly`.\n- Each item is an icon-only `HButton` (width 80, radius 8, no shadow);\n  selected = `primary`/`onPrimary`, unselected = `secondary`/`onSecondary`.\n- Pages pass their own `currentIndex`; navigation via `context.go`.\n\n### 8.5 `HTaskTile` + `TaskSheet` — list item & its bottom sheet\n\nTile: white card, radius 16, soft shadow (2px outer padding so the ripple\nclips correctly), `ListTile` with:\n- Leading: 40px-wide tap area containing an **18px circular checkbox**\n  (2px border; checked = `primary` fill + `primary` border, unchecked =\n  `#AAAAAA` border, transparent fill). Tapping it toggles.\n- Title: `bodyLarge`, single line, ellipsis. Completed → strikethrough +\n  `onSurface @ 0.5`.\n- Trailing: `HAvatar` (size 36) of the task's author.\n- Tapping the body opens the **details bottom sheet** (§9.4), which hosts\n  view / edit / delete-confirm as internal state of one sheet.\n\nState pattern: toggle is **optimistic** — flip immediately, revert and show a\n`SnackBar` on failure (`showTaskError`).\n\n### 8.6 `HAvatar` — procedural face avatars\n\nRenders an inline SVG (80×80 viewBox): colored circle + two rounded-square\neyes + a bezier mouth. Colors arrive from the API as hex strings **without**\n`#`. Sizes in use: 36 (task tile), 40 (app bar), 60 (member row), 100\n(default), 120 (settings). Fallback: `sadGrayAvatar` (gray `999` bg,\n`424242` face, downward mouth) when `avatar` is null.\n\n### 8.7 `HWeekCalendar`\n\nWhite card, radius 16, soft shadow, padding 8; 7 day cells (M T W T F S S,\nMonday-first) sized by `LayoutBuilder` with 8px spacing; selected cell =\n`primary` bg + `onPrimary` `labelMedium` text, unselected = `surface` bg +\n`onSecondary` text; cell radius 12.\n\n### 8.8 `HOfflineCard` — offline / page-error banner\n\nRed `#E94D4D` card, radius 16, soft shadow, padding 16, shown **under the\napp bar** (outer padding `fromLTRB(24, 24, 24, 0)`), containing: leading icon\n(20, white), message (`titleSmall`, white, `Expanded`), optional retry icon\n(`rotate_cw`, 22, white, 8-radius tap target). Icons: `wifi_off` when the\ndevice is offline, `cloud_off` for fetch errors.\n\n### 8.9 `SettingsAvatarButton`\n\nThe user's `HAvatar` (40) as the app bar's only action, right padding 24,\ntaps to `/settings`. Shows the fallback avatar while loading, empty box on\nerror (with a `not_authenticated` self-refresh).\n\n### 8.10 Guards (non-visual)\n\n`AuthGuard` (session errors → `/welcome`) and `HomeGuard` (wraps AuthGuard;\n`user_not_in_house` → `/create_home`). Wrap page bodies that require a\nsession/home. They listen and redirect; they don't render UI.\n\n---\n\n## 9. Page templates (recipes)\n\n### 9.1 The brand app bar — every page has it\n\n```dart\nAppBar(\n  title: HTitle(text: \"Homies\", style: context.texts.titleLarge),\n  actions: const [SettingsAvatarButton()],   // only when logged in\n  automaticallyImplyLeading: false,          // when there's no back target\n  scrolledUnderElevation: 1,\n  surfaceTintColor: context.colors.surface,\n  backgroundColor: context.colors.surface,\n  shadowColor: context.colors.onSurface.withValues(alpha: 0.25),\n)\n```\n\nThe app bar always says **\"Homies.\"** — page identity lives in the body's\n`HTitle`, not the app bar. `Scaffold.backgroundColor` is always `surface`.\n\n### 9.2 Main tab page (home, todo, calendar, wallet)\n\n```\nScaffold\n ├─ brand AppBar (+ avatar action)\n ├─ bottomNavigationBar: HNavBar(currentIndex: n)\n └─ body: Column\n     ├─ if (offline || error) HOfflineCard  [padding fromLTRB(24,24,24,0)]\n     └─ Expanded(\n         RefreshIndicator(\n           onRefresh: () => ref.refresh(provider.future),\n           child: <content>.when(  // AsyncValue\n             data:    ListView with AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics, padding 24\n             loading: Center(CircularProgressIndicator)\n             error:   keep area scrollable-but-empty (the card reports it)\n           )))\n```\n\nPull-to-refresh must keep working in every state → error/empty bodies are\nstill scrollable `ListView`s with `AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics`.\n\n### 9.3 Flow / form page (register, login, create/join home, invite)\n\n```\nScaffold(brand AppBar [+ avatar]) → SafeArea → Center → SingleChildScrollView(padding 24)\n └─ Column(start, center)\n     ├─ HTitle(text: \"...\")            // headlineLarge, may contain \\n\n     ├─ SizedBox(12)\n     ├─ Text(subtitle, displaySmall)\n     ├─ SizedBox(24)\n     ├─ HLabeledInput ...              // 8 between fields\n     ├─ SizedBox(24)\n     ├─ async.when(\n     │    data:    HButton(primary CTA)\n     │    loading: HButton(primary, loading: true)\n     │    error:   [red titleSmall message, 24] + HButton(\"Try Again\", primary))\n     ├─ SizedBox(12)\n     └─ HButton(secondary alternate CTA)   // \"I already have an account\", \"Join a Home\", \"Cancel\"\n```\n\n### 9.4 Bottom sheet / modal\n\n```dart\nshowModalBottomSheet(\n  context: context,\n  isScrollControlled: true,\n  showDragHandle: true,\n  shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(\n    borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(24)),\n  ),\n  backgroundColor: context.colors.surface,\n  barrierColor: context.colors.onSurface.withAlpha(120),\n  builder: (_) => ...,\n)\n```\n\nSheet body: `SafeArea → SingleChildScrollView(padding: bottom\nviewInsets) → Padding(fromLTRB(24, 12, 24, 24))`. Title is `headlineSmall`.\nActions row: destructive `HButton` first, cancel/secondary second, 12px gaps,\n`Expanded` for full-width text buttons.\n\nConfirmation pattern (leave home, delete task): show the question\n(`headlineSmall`, `\\n` breaks allowed) → supporting line (`bodyLarge`) →\n**destructive button on `error`, cancel on `secondary`**. While the async\naction runs: swap to loading buttons, keep a faded cancel (`secondary\n.withAlpha(120)`, no onTap), and block pop with `PopScope`.\n\n### 9.5 Loading page\n\n`Scaffold(surface bg) → Center → HTitle(\"Sto Caricando\")` or a centered\n`CircularProgressIndicator` inside content areas. *(The \"Sto Caricando\"\nstring is Italian — see §13.2.)*\n\n---\n\n## 10. State-handling patterns\n\nThese are part of the design: every async surface must handle all three\nstates.\n\n1. **Riverpod `AsyncValue.when(data/loading/error)`** everywhere; pages also\n   watch `connectivityProvider`.\n2. **Buttons absorb loading**: `HButton(loading: true)` in place of the CTA —\n   no separate spinners next to buttons.\n3. **Form errors** render as `titleSmall` red text above the CTA, and the CTA\n   relabels to **\"Try Again\"**.\n4. **Page-level errors/offline** use the `HOfflineCard` recipe (§8.8); the\n   content below stays interactive/scrollable.\n5. **Optimistic updates** for snappy toggles (task complete), with revert +\n   `SnackBar` on failure.\n6. **`PopScope`** blocks back-navigation while a submit is in flight.\n7. Redirects on auth/home state changes go through the guards / listeners\n   (`ref.listen`), never inside `when(data:)` builders.\n8. When a refresh is in flight, ignore the stale error the `AsyncValue`\n   still carries (`isLoading ? null : error`).\n\n---\n\n## 11. Motion & feel\n\n- **Bouncing scroll physics on all platforms** (`AppScrollBehavior` in\n  `main.dart`). Don't override with clamping physics.\n- **Pull-to-refresh everywhere** lists can go stale.\n- **Ripples stay inside rounded shapes**: `InkWell`/`ListTile` get a matching\n  `borderRadius`; if a shadow-clipped container eats the ripple, apply the\n  outer-container wrapper trick (§6, rule 3).\n- No animations beyond Material defaults today; if you add one, keep it\n  short (≤ 200 ms), ease-out, and offset/opacity only.\n- Tapping empty space dismisses the keyboard: pages wrap content in\n  `GestureDetector(onTap: () => FocusScope.of(context).unfocus())`, and\n  `HButton` unfocuses before firing.\n\n---\n\n## 12. Copy & tone\n\nLanguage of the UI is **English** (code comments/notes may be Italian).\n\n- Informal, warm, a little cheeky. Human words, not system words.\n- Existing examples to match:\n  - \"You and your homies have never been so organized.\"\n  - \"Do you still remember your password?\"\n  - \"First of all, let's choose a name\"\n  - \"Do you know these folks?\"\n  - \"This could be permanent, or it could be not. Who knows.\"\n  - \"They use this code to join!\"\n- **Sentence case** for buttons and titles (\"Start Now\" is the one\n  title-case exception). Never ALL CAPS, never Title Case body text.\n- Buttons are verbs: \"Sign In\", \"Create\", \"Join\", \"Try Again\",\n  \"Confirm and Leave\", \"Mark as Incomplete\".\n- Hints are playful, not literal: \"Splarpo\", \"Pa$$w0rd\", \"Gli Splarponi\",\n  \"XXXXXX\".\n- Inline validation: \"Cannot be empty.\"\n- Brand is always written \"Homies\" — and as a title always via `HTitle`\n  (\"Homies.\").\n\n### 12.1 Known copy deviations (fix opportunistically)\n\n- Loading page shows Italian \"Sto Caricando\".\n- `Colors.grey` copy icon on the invite page instead of an `onSecondary`-tone\n  token.\n\n---\n\n## 13. Naming & code conventions\n\n- Shared UI components: `H` prefix + `h_snake_case.dart` (`HButton` →\n  `h_button.dart`). Page-specific widgets may skip the prefix\n  (`NewTaskTile`, `SettingsAvatarButton`, `TaskSheet`, guards).\n- Always read theme via the extension: `context.colors`, `context.texts`\n  (`lib/extensions/theme_extension.dart`).\n- Keep layering per AGENTS.md: `data/ → providers/ → ui/{pages,components}`;\n  a new reusable widget goes in `ui/components/` only when used by 2+ pages\n  (or when it's clearly a design-system piece like an `H*` widget).\n- Semicolon-terminated, brace-heavy style in Go doesn't apply here; match the\n  surrounding Dart file.\n- Run `flutter analyze` **and** `dart run custom_lint` before considering UI\n  work done.\n\n---\n\n## 14. Checklist — adding a new element\n\nRun through this before opening a PR/committing any new UI:\n\n- [ ] Colors only from `context.colors` (new token → `theme.dart` first).\n- [ ] Text styles only from `context.texts` (+ `copyWith` for color only).\n- [ ] Icons only from `flutter_lucide`, size 24 (20 compact), snake_case.\n- [ ] Radii ∈ {8, 12, 16, 24} — default 16.\n- [ ] Spacing on the 4/8/12/16/24/32/48 ladder; screen margin 24.\n- [ ] Floating surface = `surfaceContainer` + the standard soft shadow.\n- [ ] Buttons = `HButton` variants; inputs = `HLabeledInput` / borderless\n      `TextField` inside cards.\n- [ ] Page: brand app bar \"Homies.\" + `HTitle` in body + `surface` bg.\n- [ ] All three async states handled (loading button, error text or\n      `HOfflineCard`, data).\n- [ ] Copy: playful sentence-case English, verb-first buttons.\n- [ ] Bouncing physics; pull-to-refresh if the page shows fresh data.\n- [ ] Keyboard dismissal on tap-outside / button press.\n- [ ] Destructive actions confirm via the bottom-sheet recipe (§9.4).\n- [ ] File name & class name follow §13; `flutter analyze` +\n      `dart run custom_lint` pass.\n\n---\n\n## 15. Known design debt (do not copy, do not extend)\n\n| Item | Where | Note |\n|---|---|---|\n| Two reds | `error #B94E4E` vs offline `#E94D4D` | Deliberate (banner is louder), but new destructive UI uses `error`. |\n| `Colors.grey` icon | invite page copy icon | Should be an `onSecondary`-ish token. |\n| Italian loading string | `loading_page.dart` | Should be English to match UI copy. |\n| `titleMedium` / `bodyMedium` unused | `theme.dart` | Reserved; don't repurpose them for something off-scale. |\n| Calendar & wallet pages empty | `ui/pages/` | Build them on the tab-page recipe (§9.2). |\n| Welcome app bar unstyled | `welcome_page.dart` | Missing the full app bar styling set of §9.1. |\n\n---\n\n*Maintained alongside the code. When you change `theme.dart` or an `H*`\ncomponent, update the relevant section here in the same commit.*\n"
}

1: # Homies App — Design Rules & Specification
2: 
3: This document is the single source of truth for the app's visual language.
4: Use it whenever you build a new screen, component, or interaction so it blends
5: with the existing look & feel. It is derived 1:1 from the code as of
6: **November 2026** — when the code and this document disagree, fix one of them
7: in the same change.
8: 
9: Source of truth for tokens: `lib/ui/theme.dart` (colors + typography) and
10: `lib/ui/components/` (component anatomy).
11: 
12: ---
13: 
14: ## 1. Design DNA at a glance
15: 
16: - **Friendly, playful, slightly irreverent.** A shared-housing app for
17:   "homies", not a corporate tool. Rounded shapes, warm yellow, soft shadows,
18:   cheeky copy.
19: - **Flat colors + one soft shadow.** No gradients, no glassmorphism, no
20:   layered elevation. Depth comes from a single subtle drop shadow used
21:   consistently everywhere.
22: - **White cards floating on a light-gray background.**
23: - **Light mode only.** No dark theme exists; do not add one without a
24:   project-level decision.
25: - **Yellow is the accent, not the background.** The brand yellow appears as
26:   the dot after titles, on primary buttons, and on selected states.
27: - **Every title ends with a yellow dot** (the `HTitle` signature).
28: 
29: ---
30: 
31: ## 2. Color system
32: 
33: | Token | Hex | Role |
34: |---|---|---|
35: | `primary` | `#FFC342` | Homies yellow. Primary buttons, selected nav item, selected calendar day, checked checkbox, the `HTitle` dot. |
36: | `onPrimary` | `#202020` | Text/icons on primary. |
37: | `secondary` | `#E5E5E5` | Light gray. Secondary ("quiet") buttons, unselected nav items. |
38: | `onSecondary` | `#CC202020` | Text/icons on secondary (≈ 80% black). |
39: | `surface` | `#F6F6F6` | App background, app bar background, bottom sheet background, unselected calendar day cells. |
40: | `onSurface` | `#202020` | Default text/icons, checkbox borders when checked, shadows (derived, see below). |
41: | `surfaceContainer` | `#FFFFFF` | Cards, input fields, navbar — anything that "floats" on the background. |
42: | `error` | `#B94E4E` | Muted red. Error messages, destructive buttons ("Delete", "Leave Home"). |
43: | `onError` | `#FFFFFF` | Text/icons on error. |
44: 
45: ### 2.1 Off-token colors (documented exceptions)
46: 
47: These raw colors exist in code outside the theme. Reuse them for the same
48: purpose; do not introduce new raw colors.
49: 
50: | Hex | Where | Purpose |
51: |---|---|---|
52: | `#E94D4D` | `HOfflineCard.errorBackground` | Brighter red for the offline/error banner (louder than `error` on purpose — it must grab attention). |
53: | `#AAAAAA` | `HTaskTile` unchecked checkbox border | Disabled/unchecked border tone. |
54: | `Colors.transparent` | checkbox fill (unchecked), ink overlays | — |
55: | `Colors.white` | `HOfflineCard` icons/text | Same value as `onError`; write `onError`-style intent as "white on red". |
56: 
57: ### 2.2 Opacity conventions (derived from `onSurface` / `onSecondary`)
58: 
59: | Opacity | Used for |
60: |---|---|
61: | `onSurface @ 0.25` | Every shadow (see §6). Also the app bar `shadowColor`. |
62: | `onSurface @ 0.5` (`withValues(alpha: 0.5)`) | Completed-task text + strikethrough color. |
63: | `onSecondary @ 0.6` | Input hint text. |
64: | `onSurface @ withAlpha(120)` (≈ 47%) | Bottom-sheet barrier color. |
65: | `secondary @ withAlpha(120)` | Disabled-feeling cancel button while an operation is in flight (see `LeaveConfirmationModal`). |
66: 
67: ### 2.3 Rules
68: 
69: 1. **Never hardcode a hex in a widget.** Use `context.colors.<token>` (the
70:    `ThemeContext` extension from `lib/extensions/theme_extension.dart`). New
71:    colors go into `theme.dart` first.
72: 2. Text is always `onSurface`, `onPrimary`, `onSecondary`, `onError`, or
73:    `error` — never `primary` (yellow fails contrast for text; it is a fill
74:    color, and the `HTitle` dot is its only "text" use).
75: 3. Contrast pairs are fixed: `primary↔onPrimary`, `secondary↔onSecondary`,
76:    `error↔onError`, white card ↔ `onSurface`.
77: 
78: ---
79: 
80: ## 3. Typography
81: 
82: **Font: Poppins** — bundled at weights 400 (Regular), 500 (Medium),
83: 600 (SemiBold), 700 (Bold). Declared in `pubspec.yaml`, set globally in
84: `theme.dart`. Never use another font family, and never a weight outside
85: 400–700.
86: 
87: Access via `context.texts.<style>`; override with `.copyWith(...)` only for
88: color/decoration, not size.
89: 
90: ### 3.1 Scale
91: 
92: | Style | Size / weight | Line height | Role |
93: |---|---|---|---|
94: | `displayLarge` | 52 / bold | default | Splash/hero brand title (welcome screen only). |
95: | `displaySmall` | 20 / w500 | default | **Page subtitle** — the friendly line under every `HTitle`. |
96: | `headlineLarge` | 42 / bold | 1.0 | **Page main title** (`HTitle` default) and big display values (invite code). |
97: | `headlineMedium` | 28 / bold | default | Section title inside a page ("Today's Tasks"). |
98: | `headlineSmall` | 24 / bold | default | Bottom-sheet / modal titles. |
99: | `titleLarge` | 16 / w500 | default | **App bar title** ("Homies."), user name on settings. |
100: | `titleMedium` | 16 / bold | default | *(defined, currently unused — reserve for 16px bold inline titles).* |
101: | `titleSmall` | 14 / w600 | default | **Input labels, inline error messages, banner text.** |
102: | `bodyLarge` | 16 / w400 | default | **Default body text**, input field content, hints, task text. |
103: | `bodyMedium` | 14 / w400 | default | *(defined, currently unused — reserve for dense secondary body text).* |
104: | `labelLarge` | 16 / w600 | default | **Button text** (`HButton`). |
105: | `labelMedium` | 14 / w600 | default | Small UI labels (calendar day letters). |
106: 
107: ### 3.2 Rules
108: 
109: 1. A page hierarchy is: `HTitle` (headlineLarge) → subtitle (displaySmall) →
110:    section titles (headlineMedium) → body (bodyLarge). Do not invent levels.
111: 2. Input hints use `bodyLarge` **with the hint color override** (§2.2), not a
112:    smaller size.
113: 3. Error messages are always `titleSmall` + `error` color.
114: 4. Buttons are always `labelLarge`; never resize button text.
115: 5. Multi-word H1s break with an explicit `\n` in the string
116:    ("Welcome\ninto Homies"), not automatic wrapping, so the break is witty.
117: 
118: ---
119: 
120: ## 4. Iconography
121: 
122: - **Package: `flutter_lucide`** (Lucide set). Icon identifiers are
123:   **snake_case** (`LucideIcons.list_todo`, `LucideIcons.user_round`).
124: - Material icons are only for system widgets that draw their own (e.g. the
125:   progress indicator); never pick a Material icon for UI semantics.
126: - Sizes: **24** is the default (inputs, buttons, tiles); **20** for compact
127:   spots (offline card, small icon buttons); **22** for the retry icon.
128: - Icon color follows the container's `on*` color.
129: - Icons in use today: `house`, `list_todo`, `calendar`, `wallet` (nav);
130:   `user_round`, `lock` (auth inputs); `eye` / `eye_closed` (obscure toggle);
131:   `trash`, `square_pen` (task actions); `plus` (new task); `wifi_off`,
132:   `cloud_off` (offline); `rotate_cw` (retry); `copy` (invite code).
133:   Stay within the Lucide style: 2px stroke, rounded caps, no filled icons.
134: 
135: ---
136: 
137: ## 5. Layout & spacing
138: 
139: **Base unit: 4.** Allowed steps: 4, 8, 12, 16, 24, 32, 36, 48, 80. If a value
140: isn't on this ladder, reconsider it.
141: 
142: | Distance | Value |
143: |---|---|
144: | Screen/page margin | **24** all sides (`EdgeInsets.all(24)`) |
145: | `HTitle` → subtitle | **12** |
146: | Subtitle → form/content block | **24** |
147: | Between input fields | **8** (plus each label's 2px bottom padding) |
148: | Primary button → secondary button | **12** |
149: | Between list tiles / cards | **12** |
150: | Section title → content (home) | **12** |
151: | H1 → content blocks (home) | **32–48** (roomier at the top of a page) |
152: | Card inner padding | **16** (offline card), **8** (week calendar), **2+ListTile** (task tile) |
153: | Bottom-sheet inner padding | `fromLTRB(24, 12, 24, 24)` |
154: | Icon → adjacent text inside a button | **8** |
155: | Small icon → text rows (offline card, invite) | **12** |
156: 
157: ### 5.1 Page structure conventions
158: 
159: - **Tab pages** (home, todo): `Scaffold` with brand app bar, content
160:   `Column`, `HNavBar` as `bottomNavigationBar`. Content lists pad 24; the
161:   horizontal padding may sit on the inner `Column` (todo) or the `ListView`
162:   (home) — keep 24 either way.
163: - **Flow pages** (auth, create/join home): `SafeArea → Center →
164:   SingleChildScrollView(padding: 24) → Column(crossAxisAlignment:
165:   start, mainAxisAlignment: center)`. The form is vertically centered on
166:   large screens and scrolls on small ones.
167: - Full-width stretch: cards and buttons use `width: double.infinity`.
168: 
169: ---
170: 
171: ## 6. Elevation — the one shadow
172: 
173: Every floating element (buttons, cards, inputs, navbar, app bar) uses the
174: **same** soft shadow. This is the app's signature depth cue.
175: 
176: ```dart
177: BoxShadow(
178:   color: context.colors.onSurface.withValues(alpha: 0.25),
179:   spreadRadius: 0,
180:   blurRadius: 2,
181:   offset: Offset(0, 1),
182: )
183: ```
184: 
185: App bar equivalent:
186: 
187: ```dart
188: AppBar(
189:   scrolledUnderElevation: 1,
190:   surfaceTintColor: context.colors.surface, // no M3 tint
191:   backgroundColor: context.colors.surface,
192:   shadowColor: context.colors.onSurface.withValues(alpha: 0.25),
193: )
194: ```
195: 
196: Rules:
197: 1. One shadow per element. No stacked shadows, no elevation numbers beyond
198:    this recipe.
199: 2. Compact buttons inside another surface (nav items, `NewTaskTile`'s plus,
200:    sheet action squares) set `shadow: false`.
201: 3. A shadow that gets clipped by rounded corners goes on an outer
202:    `Container` while the inner widget keeps the tap ripple (see the task
203:    tile's 2px-padding wrapper trick).
204: 
205: ---
206: 
207: ## 7. Shape / corner radius
208: 
209: | Radius | Used on |
210: |---|---|
211: | **8** | Small & icon buttons, nav item buttons, retry tap target |
212: | **12** | Calendar day cells |
213: | **16** | **Default**: cards, inputs, standard buttons, tap targets that wrap content |
214: | **24** (top corners only) | Bottom sheets: `BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(24))` |
215: | **circle / square** | Checkbox is an 18px circle; avatar eyes are rounded squares (SVG) |
216: 
217: New element? Start at 16. Go to 8 only for compact controls, 24 only for
218: sheet-style surfaces. The navbar container itself is square (radius 0) — it
219: spans the screen edge-to-edge.
220: 
221: ---
222: 
223: ## 8. Component catalog
224: 
225: All shared UI lives in `lib/ui/components/` and is prefixed **`H`** with a
226: matching `h_*.dart` filename. Reuse these before building anything new.
227: 
228: ### 8.1 `HButton` — the only button
229: 
230: Full-width by default (`width: double.infinity`), **48** tall, radius 16,
231: soft shadow, `TextButton` under the hood.
232: 
233: Key API: `text`, `icon`, `iconSize` (24), `color` *(required — variant is
234: explicit, not named)*, `textColor`, `width`, `height`, `borderRadius`,
235: `shadow`, `loading`, `loadingColor`, `onPressed`.
236: 
237: Behavior baked in — preserve it in any variant:
238: - **Unfocuses the keyboard** (`FocusScope.of(context).unfocus()`) before
239:   calling `onPressed`.
240: - `loading: true` swaps content for a 24×24 `CircularProgressIndicator`,
241:   strokeWidth 3, color `loadingColor ?? onPrimary`.
242: - Icon + text are centered in a row with an 8px gap.
243: 
244: Variants in use (copy these recipes):
245: 
246: | Variant | color / textColor | Other |
247: |---|---|---|
248: | Primary CTA | `primary` / `onPrimary` | defaults |
249: | Secondary / quiet CTA | `secondary` / `onSecondary` | defaults |
250: | Destructive CTA | `error` / `onError` | defaults |
251: | Sheet action square | `secondary` / `onSecondary` | `width: 48` (icon only) |
252: | Inline compact (e.g. add-task +) | `primary` | `width: 36, height: 36, borderRadius: 8, iconSize: 20, shadow: false` |
253: | Nav item | selected: `primary`/`onPrimary`, else `secondary`/`onSecondary` | `width: 80, borderRadius: 8, shadow: false`, icon only |
254: 
255: Async CTA pattern: `loading` replaces the button content — the button keeps
256: its size so the layout never jumps.
257: 
258: ### 8.2 `HLabeledInput` — the only labeled text field
259: 
260: Anatomy, top to bottom:
261: 1. Label: `titleSmall`, padding `EdgeInsets.only(left: 12, bottom: 2)` —
262:    optically aligned with the field's inner left padding.
263: 2. `TextFormField`, min height 48, white fill (`surfaceContainer`), radius 16,
264:    the standard soft shadow (via `DecoratedInputBorder` wrapping an
265:    `OutlineInputBorder` with `borderSide: BorderSide.none`).
266: 3. Prefix: Lucide icon, size 24, boxed in a 48×48 area with padding
267:    `left: 12, top: 12, bottom: 12, right: 4`.
268: 4. Suffix (only when `obscurable`): a 48×48 `InkWell` (radius 16) with
269:    `eye` / `eye_closed`, shown only when the field has text.
270: 5. Text and hint: `bodyLarge`; hint colored `onSecondary @ 0.6`.
271: 6. Validation error: `titleSmall` + `error`; built-in validator rejects empty
272:    ("Cannot be empty.").
273: 7. `contentPadding: EdgeInsets.only(left: 12)` and
274:    `textAlignVertical: TextAlignVertical.center`.
275: 
276: API: `label`, `hint`, `icon`, `controller`, `keyboardType`, `obscurable`.
277: 
278: Rules: use it for every form field; playful hints are part of the style
279: ("Splarpo", "Pa$$w0rd"). For **borderless inline editing inside a card**
280: (New-task tile, task edit), use a raw `TextField` with `InputBorder.none` and
281: the same hint styling instead — that's the established pattern.
282: 
283: ### 8.3 `HTitle` — the signature title
284: 
285: Bold text + a trailing yellow period, via `RichText`. Default style
286: `headlineLarge`; override with `style:` (app bar uses `titleLarge`, welcome
287: hero uses `displayLarge`). Never place a literal yellow dot in a string —
288: use `HTitle`. Never use a colon after a title.
289: 
290: ### 8.4 `HNavBar` — bottom tab bar
291: 
292: - Exactly 4 tabs: Home `/` (`house`), Todo `/todo` (`list_todo`),
293:   Calendar `/calendar` (`calendar`), Wallet `/wallet` (`wallet`).
294:   Tab order and icons are fixed.
295: - White container (`surfaceContainer`) with the soft shadow, padding 12 all
296:   around, `SafeArea` inside, items spread `spaceEvenly`.
297: - Each item is an icon-only `HButton` (width 80, radius 8, no shadow);
298:   selected = `primary`/`onPrimary`, unselected = `secondary`/`onSecondary`.
299: - Pages pass their own `currentIndex`; navigation via `context.go`.
300: 
301: ### 8.5 `HTaskTile` + `TaskSheet` — list item & its bottom sheet
302: 
303: Tile: white card, radius 16, soft shadow (2px outer padding so the ripple
304: clips correctly), `ListTile` with:
305: - Leading: 40px-wide tap area containing an **18px circular checkbox**
306:   (2px border; checked = `primary` fill + `primary` border, unchecked =
307:   `#AAAAAA` border, transparent fill). Tapping it toggles.
308: - Title: `bodyLarge`, single line, ellipsis. Completed → strikethrough +
309:   `onSurface @ 0.5`.
310: - Trailing: `HAvatar` (size 36) of the task's author.
311: - Tapping the body opens the **details bottom sheet** (§9.4), which hosts
312:   view / edit / delete-confirm as internal state of one sheet.
313: 
314: State pattern: toggle is **optimistic** — flip immediately, revert and show a
315: `SnackBar` on failure (`showTaskError`).
316: 
317: ### 8.6 `HAvatar` — procedural face avatars
318: 
319: Renders an inline SVG (80×80 viewBox): colored circle + two rounded-square
320: eyes + a bezier mouth. Colors arrive from the API as hex strings **without**
321: `#`. Sizes in use: 36 (task tile), 40 (app bar), 60 (member row), 100
322: (default), 120 (settings). Fallback: `sadGrayAvatar` (gray `999` bg,
323: `424242` face, downward mouth) when `avatar` is null.
324: 
325: ### 8.7 `HWeekCalendar`
326: 
327: White card, radius 16, soft shadow, padding 8; 7 day cells (M T W T F S S,
328: Monday-first) sized by `LayoutBuilder` with 8px spacing; selected cell =
329: `primary` bg + `onPrimary` `labelMedium` text, unselected = `surface` bg +
330: `onSecondary` text; cell radius 12.
331: 
332: ### 8.8 `HOfflineCard` — offline / page-error banner
333: 
334: Red `#E94D4D` card, radius 16, soft shadow, padding 16, shown **under the
335: app bar** (outer padding `fromLTRB(24, 24, 24, 0)`), containing: leading icon
336: (20, white), message (`titleSmall`, white, `Expanded`), optional retry icon
337: (`rotate_cw`, 22, white, 8-radius tap target). Icons: `wifi_off` when the
338: device is offline, `cloud_off` for fetch errors.
339: 
340: ### 8.9 `SettingsAvatarButton`
341: 
342: The user's `HAvatar` (40) as the app bar's only action, right padding 24,
343: taps to `/settings`. Shows the fallback avatar while loading, empty box on
344: error (with a `not_authenticated` self-refresh).
345: 
346: ### 8.10 Guards (non-visual)
347: 
348: `AuthGuard` (session errors → `/welcome`) and `HomeGuard` (wraps AuthGuard;
349: `user_not_in_house` → `/create_home`). Wrap page bodies that require a
350: session/home. They listen and redirect; they don't render UI.
351: 
352: ---
353: 
354: ## 9. Page templates (recipes)
355: 
356: ### 9.1 The brand app bar — every page has it
357: 
358: ```dart
359: AppBar(
360:   title: HTitle(text: "Homies", style: context.texts.titleLarge),
361:   actions: const [SettingsAvatarButton()],   // only when logged in
362:   automaticallyImplyLeading: false,          // when there's no back target
363:   scrolledUnderElevation: 1,
364:   surfaceTintColor: context.colors.surface,
365:   backgroundColor: context.colors.surface,
366:   shadowColor: context.colors.onSurface.withValues(alpha: 0.25),
367: )
368: ```
369: 
370: The app bar always says **"Homies."** — page identity lives in the body's
371: `HTitle`, not the app bar. `Scaffold.backgroundColor` is always `surface`.
372: 
373: ### 9.2 Main tab page (home, todo, calendar, wallet)
374: 
375: ```
376: Scaffold
377:  ├─ brand AppBar (+ avatar action)
378:  ├─ bottomNavigationBar: HNavBar(currentIndex: n)
379:  └─ body: Column
380:      ├─ if (offline || error) HOfflineCard  [padding fromLTRB(24,24,24,0)]
381:      └─ Expanded(
382:          RefreshIndicator(
383:            onRefresh: () => ref.refresh(provider.future),
384:            child: <content>.when(  // AsyncValue
385:              data:    ListView with AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics, padding 24
386:              loading: Center(CircularProgressIndicator)
387:              error:   keep area scrollable-but-empty (the card reports it)
388:            )))
389: ```
390: 
391: Pull-to-refresh must keep working in every state → error/empty bodies are
392: still scrollable `ListView`s with `AlwaysScrollableScrollPhysics`.
393: 
394: ### 9.3 Flow / form page (register, login, create/join home, invite)
395: 
396: ```
397: Scaffold(brand AppBar [+ avatar]) → SafeArea → Center → SingleChildScrollView(padding 24)
398:  └─ Column(start, center)
399:      ├─ HTitle(text: "...")            // headlineLarge, may contain \n
400:      ├─ SizedBox(12)
401:      ├─ Text(subtitle, displaySmall)
402:      ├─ SizedBox(24)
403:      ├─ HLabeledInput ...              // 8 between fields
404:      ├─ SizedBox(24)
405:      ├─ async.when(
406:      │    data:    HButton(primary CTA)
407:      │    loading: HButton(primary, loading: true)
408:      │    error:   [red titleSmall message, 24] + HButton("Try Again", primary))
409:      ├─ SizedBox(12)
410:      └─ HButton(secondary alternate CTA)   // "I already have an account", "Join a Home", "Cancel"
411: ```
412: 
413: ### 9.4 Bottom sheet / modal
414: 
415: ```dart
416: showModalBottomSheet(
417:   context: context,
418:   isScrollControlled: true,
419:   showDragHandle: true,
420:   shape: const RoundedRectangleBorder(
421:     borderRadius: BorderRadius.vertical(top: Radius.circular(24)),
422:   ),
423:   backgroundColor: context.colors.surface,
424:   barrierColor: context.colors.onSurface.withAlpha(120),
425:   builder: (_) => ...,
426: )
427: ```
428: 
429: Sheet body: `SafeArea → SingleChildScrollView(padding: bottom
430: viewInsets) → Padding(fromLTRB(24, 12, 24, 24))`. Title is `headlineSmall`.
431: Actions row: destructive `HButton` first, cancel/secondary second, 12px gaps,
432: `Expanded` for full-width text buttons.
433: 
434: Confirmation pattern (leave home, delete task): show the question
435: (`headlineSmall`, `\n` breaks allowed) → supporting line (`bodyLarge`) →
436: **destructive button on `error`, cancel on `secondary`**. While the async
437: action runs: swap to loading buttons, keep a faded cancel (`secondary
438: .withAlpha(120)`, no onTap), and block pop with `PopScope`.
439: 
440: ### 9.5 Loading page
441: 
442: `Scaffold(surface bg) → Center → HTitle("Sto Caricando")` or a centered
443: `CircularProgressIndicator` inside content areas. *(The "Sto Caricando"
444: string is Italian — see §13.2.)*
445: 
446: ---
447: 
448: ## 10. State-handling patterns
449: 
450: These are part of the design: every async surface must handle all three
451: states.
452: 
453: 1. **Riverpod `AsyncValue.when(data/loading/error)`** everywhere; pages also
454:    watch `connectivityProvider`.
455: 2. **Buttons absorb loading**: `HButton(loading: true)` in place of the CTA —
456:    no separate spinners next to buttons.
457: 3. **Form errors** render as `titleSmall` red text above the CTA, and the CTA
458:    relabels to **"Try Again"**.
459: 4. **Page-level errors/offline** use the `HOfflineCard` recipe (§8.8); the
460:    content below stays interactive/scrollable.
461: 5. **Optimistic updates** for snappy toggles (task complete), with revert +
462:    `SnackBar` on failure.
463: 6. **`PopScope`** blocks back-navigation while a submit is in flight.
464: 7. Redirects on auth/home state changes go through the guards / listeners
465:    (`ref.listen`), never inside `when(data:)` builders.
466: 8. When a refresh is in flight, ignore the stale error the `AsyncValue`
467:    still carries (`isLoading ? null : error`).
468: 
469: ---
470: 
471: ## 11. Motion & feel
472: 
473: - **Bouncing scroll physics on all platforms** (`AppScrollBehavior` in
474:   `main.dart`). Don't override with clamping physics.
475: - **Pull-to-refresh everywhere** lists can go stale.
476: - **Ripples stay inside rounded shapes**: `InkWell`/`ListTile` get a matching
477:   `borderRadius`; if a shadow-clipped container eats the ripple, apply the
478:   outer-container wrapper trick (§6, rule 3).
479: - No animations beyond Material defaults today; if you add one, keep it
480:   short (≤ 200 ms), ease-out, and offset/opacity only.
481: - Tapping empty space dismisses the keyboard: pages wrap content in
482:   `GestureDetector(onTap: () => FocusScope.of(context).unfocus())`, and
483:   `HButton` unfocuses before firing.
484: 
485: ---
486: 
487: ## 12. Copy & tone
488: 
489: Language of the UI is **English** (code comments/notes may be Italian).
490: 
491: - Informal, warm, a little cheeky. Human words, not system words.
492: - Existing examples to match:
493:   - "You and your homies have never been so organized."
494:   - "Do you still remember your password?"
495:   - "First of all, let's choose a name"
496:   - "Do you know these folks?"
497:   - "This could be permanent, or it could be not. Who knows."
498:   - "They use this code to join!"
499: - **Button labels are Title Case and verb-first**: "Sign In", "Sign Up",
500:   "Try Again", "Share Link", "Confirm and Leave", "Mark as Incomplete",
501:   "Leave Home". The personal alternate-CTA buttons are the exception and read
502:   like a sentence: "I don't have an account", "I already have an account".
503: - Titles capitalize the major words ("Task's Details", "Today's Tasks",
504:   "Create a Home"); full questions read as sentences ("Are you sure you
505:   want to leave …?"). Never ALL CAPS.
506: - Hints are playful, not literal: "Splarpo", "Pa$$w0rd", "Gli Splarponi",
507:   "XXXXXX".
508: - Inline validation: "Cannot be empty."
509: - Brand is always written "Homies" — and as a title always via `HTitle`
510:   ("Homies.").
511: 
512: ### 12.1 Known copy deviations (fix opportunistically)
513: 
514: - Loading page shows Italian "Sto Caricando".
515: - `Colors.grey` copy icon on the invite page instead of an `onSecondary`-tone
516:   token.
517: 
518: ---
519: 
520: ## 13. Naming & code conventions
521: 
522: - Shared UI components: `H` prefix + `h_snake_case.dart` (`HButton` →
523:   `h_button.dart`). Page-specific widgets may skip the prefix
524:   (`NewTaskTile`, `SettingsAvatarButton`, `TaskSheet`, guards).
525: - Always read theme via the extension: `context.colors`, `context.texts`
526:   (`lib/extensions/theme_extension.dart`).
527: - Keep layering per AGENTS.md: `data/ → providers/ → ui/{pages,components}`;
528:   a new reusable widget goes in `ui/components/` only when used by 2+ pages
529:   (or when it's clearly a design-system piece like an `H*` widget).
530: - Semicolon-terminated, brace-heavy style in Go doesn't apply here; match the
531:   surrounding Dart file.
532: - Run `flutter analyze` **and** `dart run custom_lint` before considering UI
533:   work done.
534: 
535: ---
536: 
537: ## 14. Checklist — adding a new element
538: 
539: Run through this before opening a PR/committing any new UI:
540: 
541: - [ ] Colors only from `context.colors` (new token → `theme.dart` first).
542: - [ ] Text styles only from `context.texts` (+ `copyWith` for color only).
543: - [ ] Icons only from `flutter_lucide`, size 24 (20 compact), snake_case.
544: - [ ] Radii ∈ {8, 12, 16, 24} — default 16.
545: - [ ] Spacing on the 4/8/12/16/24/32/48 ladder; screen margin 24.
546: - [ ] Floating surface = `surfaceContainer` + the standard soft shadow.
547: - [ ] Buttons = `HButton` variants; inputs = `HLabeledInput` / borderless
548:       `TextField` inside cards.
549: - [ ] Page: brand app bar "Homies." + `HTitle` in body + `surface` bg.
550: - [ ] All three async states handled (loading button, error text or
551:       `HOfflineCard`, data).
552: - [ ] Copy: playful sentence-case English, verb-first buttons.
553: - [ ] Bouncing physics; pull-to-refresh if the page shows fresh data.
554: - [ ] Keyboard dismissal on tap-outside / button press.
555: - [ ] Destructive actions confirm via the bottom-sheet recipe (§9.4).
556: - [ ] File name & class name follow §13; `flutter analyze` +
557:       `dart run custom_lint` pass.
558: 
559: ---
560: 
561: ## 15. Known design debt (do not copy, do not extend)
562: 
563: | Item | Where | Note |
564: |---|---|---|
565: | Two reds | `error #B94E4E` vs offline `#E94D4D` | Deliberate (banner is louder), but new destructive UI uses `error`. |
566: | `Colors.grey` icon | invite page copy icon | Should be an `onSecondary`-ish token. |
567: | Italian loading string | `loading_page.dart` | Should be English to match UI copy. |
568: | `titleMedium` / `bodyMedium` unused | `theme.dart` | Reserved; don't repurpose them for something off-scale. |
569: | Calendar & wallet pages empty | `ui/pages/` | Build them on the tab-page recipe (§9.2). |
570: | Welcome app bar unstyled | `welcome_page.dart` | Missing the full app bar styling set of §9.1. |
571: 
572: ---

1: # Homies Tasks API — Quick Reference (for calendar integration)
2: 
3: Base URL (dev): `http://localhost:8080` · All endpoints require `Authorization: Bearer <jwt>`.
4: Rate limit: 10 req/s on `/lists`. Errors always look like:
5: `{"error": {"message": "...", "code": "<error_code>"}}` with HTTP 400 (bad input/DB) or 403 (list belongs to another house).
6: 
7: ## Data model
8: 
9: **List**
10: ```json
11: { "id": "12", "name": "Groceries" }
12: ```
13: 
14: **Item (task)**
15: ```json
16: {
17:   "id": "34",
18:   "text": "Buy milk",
19:   "completed": false,
20:   "author": "uuid-of-creator",
21:   "created_at": "2026-11-10T15:00:00Z",
22:   "due_at": "2026-11-15T18:00:00Z"
23: }
24: ```
25: - `due_at` is **optional**: an RFC3339 UTC timestamp, or `null` when the task has no due date. Always present in responses.
26: - `id`s are strings (stringified ints).
27: 
28: ## Endpoints
29: 
30: ### 1. `GET /lists/` — all lists of the user's house
31: Response: `{"lists": [List, ...]}`
32: 
33: ### 2. `GET /lists/:id/` — items of a list (paged, time-filtered)
34: Query params (all optional, combinable):
35: - `limit` — max items, default **10**, capped at 10
36: - `from` — RFC3339; only items with `created_at >= from`
37: - `to` — RFC3339; only items with `created_at < to`
38: - `due_on` — `YYYY-MM-DD`; only items whose `due_at` falls in that **UTC day**, i.e. `[YYYY-MM-DD 00:00:00Z, next day 00:00:00Z)`. Items with `due_at == null` are excluded. Invalid format → param silently ignored.
39: 
40: ⚠️ Timezone note: `due_on` matches on the **UTC** day, not the device's local day.
41: For a local-time calendar, either accept the UTC-day approximation or compute the
42: local day's UTC boundaries yourself and use `from`/`to`... note however `from`/`to`
43: filter `created_at`, not `due_at` — so for exact local-day filtering, fetch with
44: `due_on` for the UTC day before, the day itself, and the day after (or just the day
45: itself if tasks are due during daytime) and filter `due_at` client-side.
46: 
47: Response: `{"items": [Item, ...]}` — ordered by `created_at` **DESC** (newest first).
48: 
49: **Calendar example:** tasks due on Nov 15, 2026:
50: `GET /lists/34/?due_on=2026-11-15`
51: 
52: ### 3. `PUT /lists/:id/` — create item
53: Body:
54: ```json
55: { "text": "Buy milk", "due_at": "2026-11-15T18:00:00Z" }
56: ```
57: - `text`: required, 3–511 chars.
58: - `due_at`: optional. Omit or send `null` → no due date. Must be RFC3339.
59: 
60: Response: `{"id": "34"}` (id of the new item)
61: 
62: ### 4. `PATCH /lists/:id/:item_id` — update item
63: Body fields are all optional, patch-like:
64: ```json
65: { "text": "New text", "completed": true, "due_at": "2026-11-20T09:00:00Z" }
66: ```
67: Branching logic (quirky, follow exactly):
68: - **`text` non-empty** → updates `text` (+ `due_at` if present, see below). `completed` is IGNORED on this branch.
69: - **`text` empty/absent** → sets `completed` to the given value (+ `due_at` if present).
70: 
71: `due_at` semantics:
72: - field **absent** → existing due date unchanged
73: - field **`null`** → due date removed
74: - field **RFC3339 string** → due date set (validated; bad format → 400 `basic_check_error`)
75: 
76: Response: `{}`
77: 
78: ### 5. `DELETE /lists/:id/:item_id` — delete item
79: No body. Response: `{}`
80: 
81: ## Error codes (in `error.code`)
82: `json_format_error`, `basic_check_error`, `list_not_found`, `item_not_found`,
83: `cant_access_this` (403), `internal_error`, `not_authenticated`.
84: 
85: ## Calendar-relevant notes
86: - To toggle completion without touching text: `PATCH` with `{"completed": true}` (no `text` field).
87: - To set only a due date: `PATCH` with `{"due_at": "..."}` (no `text` field).
88: - Tasks without a due date (`due_at == null`) never match `due_on` and should not appear on a calendar.
89: - All timestamps are UTC (server timezone is UTC); convert to local time in the client.
90: - Pagination cap is 10 items per request and there is no offset/cursor param — a busy day with >10 due tasks in one list cannot be fully retrieved with `due_on` alone yet.
91: - There is no cross-list "all events" endpoint: iterate over `GET /lists/` and query each list.
