Always renders 6 weeks so the card height\n/// never jumps between months.\n///\n/// [markedDays] (from the due-days API) draws a dot under days that have due\n/// tasks: primary while some are still open, faint once all are completed.\n/// Tapping a leading/trailing day of an adjacent month still fires\n/// [onDaySelected] — the page decides whether to follow the month.\nclass HMonthCalendar extends StatelessWidget {\n  const HMonthCalendar({\n    super.key,\n    required this.month,\n    required this.selected,\n    required this.onDaySelected,\n    required this.onMonthChanged,\n    this.markedDays = const {},\n  });\n\n  /// Any day within the shown month; the grid derives its own bounds.\n  final DateTime month;\n\n  /// Date-only local day (see [dateOnly]).\n  final DateTime selected;\n\n  final ValueChanged<DateTime> onDaySelected;\n  final ValueChanged<DateTime> onMonthChanged;\n\n  /// Due-task marks keyed by date-only day.\n  final Map<DateTime, DueDay> markedDays;\n\n  @override\n  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n    final today = dateOnly(DateTime.now());\n    final gridStart = startOfGrid(month);\n    final cells = List.generate(\n      42, // 6 weeks — stable height across months\n      (i) => DateTime(gridStart.year, gridStart.month, gridStart.day + i),\n    );\n\n    return Container(\n      width: double.infinity,\n      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),\n      decoration: BoxDecoration(\n        color: context.colors.surfaceContainer,\n        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),\n        boxShadow: [\n          BoxShadow(\n            color: context.colors.onSurface.withValues(alpha: 0.25),\n            blurRadius: 2,\n            offset: const Offset(0, 1),\n          ),\n        ],\n      ),\n      child: Column(\n        mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,\n        children: [\n          _MonthHeader(month: month, onMonthChanged: onMonthChanged),\n          const SizedBox(height: 8),\n          LayoutBuilder(\n            builder: (context, constraints) {\n              // constraints.maxWidth is already inside the card's 8px\n              // padding. Same cell math as HWeekCalendar: six 8px gaps\n              // between 7 columns. Floored so spaceBetween can never\n              // overflow by a floating-point epsilon.\n              const spacing = 8.0;\n              final boxSize =\n                  ((constraints.maxWidth - (7 - 1) * spacing) / 7)\n                      .floorToDouble();\n\n              return Column(\n                mainAxisSize: MainAxisSize.min,\n                children: [\n                  Row(\n                    mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,\n                    children: List.generate(\n                      7,\n                      (i) => SizedBox(\n                        width: boxSize,\n                        child: Text(\n                          _weekdayLabels[i],\n                          textAlign: TextAlign.center,\n                          style: context.texts.labelMedium!.copyWith(\n                            color: context.colors.onSecondary,\n                          ),\n                        ),\n                      ),\n                    ),\n                  ),\n                  for (var week = 0; week < 6; week++) ...[\n                    const SizedBox(height: 8),\n                    Row(\n                      mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.spaceBetween,\n                      children: List.generate(\n                        7,\n                        (i) =>\n                            _DayCell(\n                              day: cells[week * 7 + i],\n                              size: boxSize,\n                              month: month,\n                              today: today,\n                              selected: selected,\n                              mark: markedDays[cells[week * 7 + i]],\n                              onTap: onDaySelected,\n                            ),\n                      ),\n                    ),\n                  ],\n                ],\n              );\n            },\n          ),\n        ],\n      ),\n    );\n  }\n}\n\nclass _MonthHeader extends StatelessWidget {\n  const _MonthHeader({required this.month, required this.onMonthChanged});\n\n  final DateTime month;\n  final ValueChanged<DateTime> onMonthChanged;\n\n  @override\n  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n    return Row(\n      children: [\n        HButton(\n          icon: LucideIcons.chevron_left,\n          iconSize: 20,\n          width: 40,\n          height: 40,\n          borderRadius: 8,\n          shadow: false,\n          color: context.colors.secondary,\n          textColor: context.colors.onSecondary,\n          onPressed: () =>\n              onMonthChanged(DateTime(month.year, month.month - 1)),\n        ),\n        Expanded(\n          child: Text(\n            formatMonth(month),\n            textAlign: TextAlign.center,\n            style: context.texts.titleMedium,\n          ),\n        ),\n        HButton(\n          icon: LucideIcons.chevron_right,\n          iconSize: 20,\n          width: 40,\n          height: 40,\n          borderRadius: 8,\n          shadow: false,\n          color: context.colors.secondary,\n          textColor: context.colors.onSecondary,\n          onPressed: () =>\n              onMonthChanged(DateTime(month.year, month.month + 1)),\n        ),\n      ],\n    );\n  }\n}\n\nclass _DayCell extends StatelessWidget {\n  const _DayCell({\n    required this.day,\n    required this.size,\n    required this.month,\n    required this.today,\n    required this.selected,\n    required this.onTap,\n    this.mark,\n  });\n\n  final DateTime day;\n  final double size;\n  final DateTime month;\n  final DateTime today;\n  final DateTime selected;\n  final DueDay? mark;\n  final ValueChanged<DateTime> onTap;\n\n  @override\n  Widget build(BuildContext context) {\n    final inMonth = day.month == month.month;\n    final isSelected = day == selected;\n    final isToday = inMonth && day == today;\n\n    final Color background;\n    final Color textColor;\n    if (isSelected) {\n      background = context.colors.primary;\n      textColor = context.colors.onPrimary;\n    } else if (inMonth) {\n      background = context.colors.surface;\n      textColor =\n          isToday ? context.colors.onSurface : context.colors.onSecondary;\n    } else {\n      // Leading/trailing days of adjacent months: no chip, dimmed number.\n      background = Colors.transparent;\n      textColor = context.colors.onSurface.withValues(alpha: 0.3);\n    }\n\n    // Open tasks get the brand yellow; a fully-checked day fades out.\n    final Color? dotColor =\n        mark == null\n            ? null\n            : isSelected\n            ? context.colors.onPrimary\n            : mark!.open > 0\n            ? context.colors.primary\n            : context.colors.onSurface.withValues(alpha: 0.25);\n\n    return SizedBox(\n      width: size,\n      height: size,\n      child: Material(\n        color: background,\n        shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(\n          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),\n          side:\n              isToday && !isSelected\n                  ? BorderSide(color: context.colors.primary, width: 2)\n                  : BorderSide.none,\n        ),\n        child: InkWell(\n          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),\n          onTap: () => onTap(day),\n          child: Column(\n            mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,\n            children: [\n              Text(\n                '${day.day}',\n                style: context.texts.labelMedium!.copyWith(color: textColor),\n              ),\n              const SizedBox(height: 2),\n              Container(\n                width: 4,\n                height: 4,\n                decoration: BoxDecoration(\n                  shape: BoxShape.circle,\n                  color: dotColor ?? Colors.transparent,\n                ),\n              ),\n            ],\n          ),\n        ),\n      ),\n    );\n  }\n}\n",
